## Cabo Verde added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cabo Verde’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **3 books, all of them student (pupil) manuals of the Ministério da Educação de Cabo Verde: *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (Edição 2019), *Manual de Matemática 5º ano* (Edição 2020) and *Matemática 7º ano — Manual do(a) aluno(a)* (Edição 2020); 0 teacher guides** — and placed 197 teaching items (Grade 3: 47, Grade 5: 69, Grade 7: 81; 391 PDF pages, every page read). The expansion map’s count of 3 student + 0 teacher is CORRECT. Set boundary, proved from the records: `textbooks.tsv` carries exactly 17 Cabo Verde rows (all Government, grades 1–7), of which exactly three are Mathematics, all student; the download list’s §C enumerates 93 ministry manuals, including Maths manuals for grades 1, 2, 4, 6, 8, 9, 10 and 11 and applied-Maths manuals — every one of those is exact-link-only (no TSV row, no staging file, no GitHub copy) and NOT in hand, so none is cited here; counting any of them would be a set error. Record gap, reported not fixed (record-side): `phase3-push-state.json` contains no Cabo Verde entry at all, and there is no Cabo Verde folder or `books.json` in phase3-staging — the in-hand evidence is the TSV’s GitHub URLs themselves, and all three GitHub copies were located, verified (byte size and MD5; the Grade 5 ministry copy is byte-identical to the GitHub copy, so that book is one book, cited once) and read directly, so the set is settled despite the push-state gap. No duplicates exist in the set: three distinct grades, three distinct books. Printed page = PDF page in all three books. The placements’ named exclusions — front matter, unit openers, objectives lists and the closing national-anthem page in the Grade 5 and Grade 7 books; the Grade 3 book’s cover plate and welcome/mascot pages (PDF pp. 1, 3, 5), its part-opener pages (PDF pp. 55, 85, 107, 111), its closing test “Teste” (printed pp. 125–129 — mixed assessment re-using taught content, no new teaching) and the flag / national-anthem page (PDF p. 130) — and pure exercise / consolidation blocks that teach nothing new are stocked nowhere. The 12 book errors found in the read (2 in the Grade 5 book, 5 in the Grade 7 book, 5 in the Grade 3 book) are carried corrected on the rows they touch. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items from Book 1 and Book 2.

**Book 1:** *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (student manual) — Cabo Verde, Ministério da Educação, Edição 2019; authors Jimmy Leppãnen, Sara Hoye, FvZ; editor Gamlin & Zeipel Communication AB; 140,731,505 bytes, 130 PDF pp., MD5 112ca4f2fc6577b4eb035b0237d9253c (read from the GitHub release copy in release `full-quality-oversized-books`; its byte size equals the ministry HEAD size recorded in the download list §C row 13 exactly). Printed page = PDF page. Pupil textbook.

**Book 2:** *Manual de Matemática 5º ano* (student manual) — Cabo Verde, Ministério da Educação, Edição 2020; authors João Duarte, Vanda Delgado; 11,054,236 bytes, 118 PDF pp., MD5 7523a460a8d971a028632077eeb0789c (the GitHub copy and the ministry copy are byte-identical — one book, cited once). Printed page = PDF page throughout the body (its unit-opener pages, PDF pp. 6–7, are unnumbered and are cited by PDF page). Pupil textbook.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Area units in the metric system (km² to mm², each 100× the next) and agrarian units: are = dam², hectare = hm² = 10,000 m² | Cabo Verde — *Manual de Matemática 5º ano* (student manual), printed pp. 108–109 |
| Length: the metre as the fundamental unit; dm, cm and mm as submultiples; ruler and tape measure; conversions and measures written as decimals of a metre | Cabo Verde — *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (student manual), printed pp. 70–72 |
| Mass: weighing on a balance; kilogram and gram (1 kg = 1000 g) and the full unit ladder kg–hg–dag–g–dg–cg–mg with ×10 / ÷10 relations | Cabo Verde — *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (student manual), printed pp. 118–119 |
| Capacity: the litre; half litre 0,5 l and quarter litre 0,25 l; dl, cl and ml as submultiples (1 l = 10 dl = 100 cl = 1000 ml); conversions | Cabo Verde — *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (student manual), printed pp. 122–124 |

Cabo Verde is added to the countries line and to the acceptance count above — 4 pupil items in this lesson, from real pupil books of the Ministério da Educação de Cabo Verde. Cabo Verde’s class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Cabo Verde Maths book (all 3) has been read in full and stocked; the link-only ministry Maths manuals for the other grades are not in hand, and Cabo Verde reopens for a grade only if one of those manuals is ever grabbed and read.

## Canada added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Canada's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **2 works in 4 files: *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide, Elementary & Secondary* (FNESC/FNSA, ©2020), 273 pp. — the one book in Canada's classified in-hand Maths set (0 student + 1 teacher) — and the borderline pupil work *I Like Math!* (Government of Nunavut, 2017), one Level 8 guided-reading fiction book held in three language editions of 11 pp. each and filed in every record under the Inuktut Ilinniaqta Early Books label, not under Maths** — and placed 117 items (112 teacher-guide evidence items from the guide, items 1–112, plus 5 pupil items from *I Like Math!*, items S1–S5). Every item from the guide is TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE: the guide directs teachers how to teach BC mathematics through First Peoples contexts, with mini-units, activities and blackline masters, and states BC curriculum connections in its tables; it expressly does not explain the mathematics or the curriculum in detail (its printed p. 35) and presumes a trained teacher. No pupil Maths textbook is in hand for Canada: Canada has no national textbook series (education is provincial/territorial), and the five provincial student textbooks in the records (Nelson *Principles of Mathematics 10*; McGraw-Hill Ryerson *Principles of Mathematics 9*; Pearson *Math Makes Sense 3, 7, 8*) stand filled-by-exact-link only — never staged, never secured, not read here and cited nowhere (a sixth, *Nelson Mathematics 8*, is a candidate only, its imprint never read). Canada is therefore cited as EVIDENCE ONLY, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent: Canada is added to this lesson's countries line and named in its rows, but counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The *I Like Math!* items are stocked as pupil evidence from that work exactly as the placements label them — 3 carry taught content (S1, S2, S4) and 2 are context/named-only (S3, S5) — and are likewise counted in no taught-in figure; whether the Nunavut early-books titles count as pupil Maths at all is a record-side question for the parent, reported here, not ruled on. The guide's Water-Keepers items (98–105) rest on the guide's printed teaching alone: the separate FNESC Water Keepers digital resource the guide points to (printed p. 224) is in no record as in hand and was not read. The guide is the 2020 revision of the 2011 *Math First Peoples* resource; the 2011 predecessor located on a Padlet host is not a separate in-hand book and is cited nowhere. The placements' named exclusions — the guide's introduction, goals/rationale and teacher-reflection material; First Peoples Principles of Learning, story protocols and respectful-practice rules; the resource-design rubric; community-visit apparatus beyond the Interview Guide's six content categories; unit-organization front matter; the cultural/historical context narratives and stories inside the mini-units (context-setting only; no story text is reproduced anywhere in the placements or here); BLM worksheets that merely repeat an itemised activity; formative/summative assessment prompts, guiding questions, video and website lists and the Additional Resources bibliographies; and the *I Like Math!* front/back matter — teach no new mathematics and are stocked nowhere. The 5 book errors found in the read (placements E1–E5) are carried corrected on the rows they touch: E1 (sphere-volume worked example, printed p. 123 — a unit slip and a garbled intermediate line inside a volume calculation; the final answer, 500 cm³, is correct) on item 56; E2 (BLM 6 conversion sheet, printed p. 185 — the percent-to-decimal rule states the decimal-point direction backwards; both printed examples are correct) on item 83; E3 (Moose Tracks answer key, printed p. 181 — mixes “less than or equal to 20” with m < 20 and marks the boundary value inconsistently) on item 71; E4 (Circle Unit Answer Key, printed p. 211 — says “smoke hole” for “fire circle”; the project instructions at printed p. 204 state it correctly) on item 92; E5 (Weaving BLM list, printed p. 55 — numbers both Keep or Toss sheets BLM 16; they are BLM 15 and BLM 16) is a list typo affecting no item's content. Printed page = PDF page in the guide throughout; *I Like Math!* citations are its printed story folios. Canada's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items (items 2, 39, 46 and S5).

**Book 1:** *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide, Elementary & Secondary* (teacher resource guide) — Canada, First Nations Education Steering Committee (FNESC) and First Nations Schools Association (FNSA), ©2020; 32,264,979 bytes (byte-exact against the record), 273 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide directs teachers how to teach BC mathematics through First Peoples contexts and states curriculum connections; it expressly does not explain the mathematics in detail (printed p. 35); it is not a pupil textbook.

**Book 2:** *I Like Math!* (pupil book) — Canada, Laura Edlund, illus. Amanda Sandland, Inhabit Education / Government of Nunavut, ©2017 (Inuinnaqtun edition ©2018); one Level 8 guided-reading fiction book in three language editions (English, ISBN 978-1-77266-349-5; Inuktitut, ISBN 978-1-77266-350-1; Inuinnaqtun, ISBN 978-1-77266-977-0), 11 PDF pages each, cited once for the work. Its own book-details page gives its subject as “Daily uses for math”; every record files it under Inuktut Ilinniaqta Early Books, not Maths. PUPIL EVIDENCE, supplementary — its items are labelled on their rows (taught / context only / named only) and counted in no taught-in figure.

|---|---|
| Investigating local measurement practice — standard and body units, tools, small/large/great distances, rate, weight, time, calendars, temperature, perimeter/area/volume (Cultural Math Interview Guide) | Canada — *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide* (FNESC/FNSA, 2020) (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed p. 30 |
| Measuring facial features in metric units and converting the dimensions to imperial to make a mask that fits | Canada — *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide* (FNESC/FNSA, 2020) (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed p. 104 |
| Maximising drum numbers from one hide: circles cut, lashing length at a constant minimum width, and waste compared as area of lashing vs area of hide minus circles, over three strategy iterations | Canada — *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide* (FNESC/FNSA, 2020) (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed p. 109 |
| Learning about measuring doors and weights at a father's workplace — pupil evidence, named only: measuring is named, but no unit, instrument or measurement is taught | Canada — *I Like Math!* (Government of Nunavut, 2017) (pupil book) — pupil evidence, printed pp. 14–15 |

Canada is added to the countries line above and named in this section's rows as EVIDENCE ONLY — teacher-guide evidence from *Math First Peoples* (and, where they land, pupil evidence from *I Like Math!*) — and is NOT added to the acceptance count: no Canada item is counted in this lesson's taught-in figure, on the Ghana evidence-only precedent. Canada's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)** — the classified in-hand set is one teacher resource guide with no pupil Maths textbook in hand; the five exact-link provincial student textbooks were not read and are cited nowhere.

## Costa Rica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Costa Rica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **25 books, all of them student books: the five CONED distance-secondary antologías *Antología de Matemáticas*, Niveles 7–11 (UNED/CONED — Nivel Sétimo 2017, Nivel Noveno 2018, Nivel 11 2019; Nivel Décimo by MSc. Jorge Díaz Porras) and the ITCR Proyecto EVEPRIM primary books for Grades 3 and 6 (10 textos + 10 folletos de prácticas across Relaciones y Álgebra, Números, Geometría, Medidas, Estadística and Probabilidad); 0 teacher guides for Costa Rica Maths exist in hand** — and placed 229 teaching items. **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 17 student is WRONG** — 17 is exactly the number of Costa Rica Mathematics rows in textbooks.tsv (10 EVEPRIM textos + only 2 of the 10 EVEPRIM folletos + 5 CONED antologías); the TSV was never updated for 8 folletos staged on 2026-10-25, and the staging books.json files and phase3-push-state.json agree on the true set of 25 (the folletos count student-side, per the map's own stated basis). All 25 were grabbed, verified (byte size + MD5 against books.json; the two Relaciones y Álgebra textos, which have no books.json entry, verified by size + title page as the main-repo files) and read in full — no partial excerpts, no duplicates (the SharePoint folder copies of the six texto books matched the staged sizes byte-for-byte, so they are the same files, cited once) and no unopenable files. The three Grade 3 textos were read in their November 2026 editions, the in-hand editions per books.json; the GitHub release mid-size-books-3 still holds the superseded October editions of those three textos (a record-level flag, reported, not fixed — same books, older exports; only the November editions are cited here). Two chapter promises in the CONED books are NOT teaching and place no item anywhere: CONED 7's Capítulo 4 is titled Estadística y Probabilidad but teaches statistics only (probability appears only in its introductory discussion questions, pp. 209–214), and CONED 10's Capítulo 1 objectives promise plane transformations that no Tema in the chapter teaches (only a recall of Grade 8 homothety). The placements' named exclusions — front matter, indices, bibliographies and answer keys (soluciones); the EVEPRIM answer-key chapters and the review chapters that re-teach already-placed sections; the CONED chapter motto/introduction pages and “desafío matemático” pages; and the videos and web interactives the EVEPRIM books link to — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The 5 CONED book-error groups found in the read — CONED 7: the angle table (p. 106) labels a 360° angle “CONVEXO”, the inverse-proportionality example (pp. 201–203) states v·t = 247.5 for a product of 330 and “very close to 3 hours” at 78 km/h for ≈4.23 h with garbled fraction work, the triangle inequality (p. 125) omits “any two” sides, the median answer (p. 238) gives 68 for a median of 67, the worked powers page (p. 64) misaligns its results, and a tiling exercise (p. 35) reads “largo por largo”; CONED 8: the table of contents numbers the equations Tema “Tema 4” while the body heads it “Tema 5” (numbering slip only); CONED 9: the SI prefix table (p. 74) gives exa the symbol “R” (exa is E), the radian-to-degree rule (p. 117) is printed inverted/garbled though its worked examples are correct, and the contents duplicates “Función Cuadrática” for Capítulo 3 Temas 1 and 4 and mislabels Capítulo 4 as “Capítulo III”; CONED 10: the falling-body model (p. 373) prints “12” for ½ (the worked values use ½ correctly), the key (p. 42) duplicates “tangentes” where “secantes” is meant, p. 14 asks for the area of a “circunferencia” (the circle), and the pentagon solution (p. 86) drops its degree symbols; CONED 11: the logarithm table (p. 118) misaligns its examples (it prints log₅ 5 = 2, which belongs to log₅ 25), the wound-model solution (p. 172) shows an unsigned division display before the correct result, and “invectiva” (p. 121) is a typo for inyectiva — are carried corrected on the rows they touch. One further note is carried as the placements carry it: the Medidas G3 answer key's building-height estimate reads 45 dam in the October copy and “4 dam” in the November extraction — treated as a re-export/extraction artefact in the key, not teaching content, and cited nowhere. Costa Rica's books are pupil books, so Costa Rica is COUNTED: it joins this lesson's acceptance count wherever the placements stock an item. CONED printed page = PDF page throughout; EVEPRIM pages are cited by PDF page with the book's section numbers (two exceptions print folios: the EVEPRIM 6 Probabilidad texto's printed folio = PDF page − 6 and the EVEPRIM 3 Medidas texto's = PDF page − 7; citations give PDF pages). Into this lesson the placements stock 10 items (items 51, 130, 192, 194, 196, 222, 224, 225, 227 and 229).

**Book 1:** *Antología de Matemáticas — Nivel Noveno* (student antología) — Costa Rica, Universidad Estatal a Distancia (UNED) / Colegio Nacional de Educación a Distancia (CONED), 2018; 9,577,886 bytes, 431 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page throughout (the two scanned cover images are PDF pp. 1–2; the title page is printed p. 3). Pupil book (distance-secondary antología).

**Book 2:** *Medidas, 6º — Folleto de prácticas* — Costa Rica, Escuela de Matemática, ITCR, Proyecto EVEPRIM 6.1; 1,476,721 bytes, 26 PDF pp.; a compilation of the practices of the Medidas texto. Pages cited by PDF page. Pupil practice book.

**Book 3:** *Medidas, 3º — Folleto de prácticas* — Costa Rica, Escuela de Matemática, ITCR, Proyecto EVEPRIM 3.2; 21,471,558 bytes, 45 PDF pp.; a compilation of the practices of the Medidas texto. Pages cited by PDF page. Pupil practice book.

**Book 4:** *Medidas, 6º* (texto) — Costa Rica, Escuela de Matemática, ITCR, Proyecto EVEPRIM 6.1; 43,833,912 bytes, 242 PDF pp. Pages are cited by PDF page with the book's own section numbers (§). Pupil textbook.

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cuba's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **29 in-hand entries = 25 distinct works (MINED, Editorial Pueblo y Educación, Cuba), every one read in full**: student textbooks for Grades 1 and 3–12, student workbooks for Grades 1, 2 and 3, and teacher documents (Programa and Orientaciones metodológicas) for Grades 1–5, 8 and 11 — and placed 247 teaching items (202 student-side, 45 teacher-evidence). **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 14 student + 8 teacher is WRONG** — the staging books.json holds 13 student + 9 teacher entries and textbooks.tsv holds 7 further secured entries; duplicated filings are cited once (the Grade 3 and Grade 8 student books were each verified in a second edition; the Grade 4 and Grade 5 student books are each filed twice as byte-identical copies — Grade 4 MD5 01c106741b186cf28d360854a40d7e93, Grade 5 MD5 b48e28b1df820e2451bb44eb33b5ac11). Record corrections the read proved, carried exactly: the record's “Matemática 2do grado Parte I student textbook” is in fact the COMPLETE Grade 2 workbook (Cuaderno de trabajo — Matemática 2, ISBN 978-959-13-1854-1) — **no Grade 2 student textbook is in hand**, and Grade 2 rests on its workbook, Programa and Orientaciones; the record's Grade 5 student byte size (36,868,096 B) is a truncated copy — the complete file read is 77,146,019 B / 396 pp; the Grade 11 teacher documents belong to the newer Perfeccionamiento programme and do **not** match the in-hand Grade 11 student book (2005 edition — its limits, derivatives and integrals chapters have no teacher companion in hand, and the programme's trigonometry unit has no chapter in that book), so they are filed as that programme's own teacher evidence, never as evidence for the 2005 book; the MINED portal source URLs now return soft-404 pages — the reads used Wayback captures of those same URLs and the GitHub routes. The placements' named exclusions — exercise sets, chapter exercises, self-evaluation sections, answers appendices, mathematical tables, historical feature essays and front/back matter — are stocked nowhere. Teacher items are TEACHER EVIDENCE — they state what the programme requires and how it is taught — are labelled as such on every row, and are never counted in this lesson's acceptance figure; student textbook and workbook items are COUNTED. Printed pages are the books' own printed folios as the placements cite them (each book's printed-page basis is stated in its book entry). ## Bulgaria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Bulgaria's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 files = 4 works, all pupil textbooks: the Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika) *Математика* for Grades 1, 2 and 3 and for Grade 4 in two part-files (1 част / 2 част), 642 PDF pages in all** — and placed 123 teaching items. **The expansion map's “5 student + 0 teacher” is CORRECT as a file count (5 files, 4 works). Full-book vs sample verdict: NO in-hand Maths file is a sample** — every file's extent matches its own imprint's print-sheet count (Grade 1: 20.5 sheets, 128 lessons to printed p. 158 + 12 appendices; Grade 2: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 3: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 4: 10 + 11.5 sheets, lessons 1–136), and the 8 proven publisher samples in Bulgaria's library record (Music G6, Foreign Language G6/G7, БЕЛ G10/G11 and Geography G5) contain no Maths book, so the library's sample ruling does not touch this set. All five PDFs are image-only with no usable text layer and were read visually from page renders (cover, imprint, the book's own contents, and body pages for every distinct topic); printed folio = PDF page − 1 in every book, and Grade 4 Part 2 restarts its own pagination (its citations are marked “Part 2”). No Bulgaria Maths book was in the course before this merge — the string “Bulgaria” occurred nowhere under `maths/` — so every placed item is new here. Set boundary: everything else Bulgarian Maths in the records is exact-link / reader only and NOT in hand — Riva Maths G1–G4 PDFs (duplicate slots for the same grades), Klett epub Maths G5/G6 readers, Klett FlipHTML5 Maths G7/G8 (fullness unproven) and Архимед Maths G10/G11 Ourboox PDFs — none is part of this read and none is cited here; if any is later secured and read, Bulgaria reverts to PARTIAL until it is stocked. The placements' named exclusions — each grade's diagnostics, self-checks and consolidation lessons, drills, blitz tournaments, games and projects (other than the Grade 4 data project stocked in the statistics lessons), end-of-unit and year reviews, and the Grade 1 book's 12 cut-out appendices (number cards, shape cut-outs, play money, tangram, clock face, addition wheel, 0–20 ruler) — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere; the topics they practise are counted at the lessons that teach them. **Book-internal flag, carried exactly: the Grade 1 book's introduction (printed p. 4) promises the unit litre, but no lesson teaches it — the contents list no litre lesson and the closing units summary (lesson 127, printed p. 155, read on the body page) lists only hour, euro, eurocent, kilogram and centimetre — so the litre is stocked nowhere, and Bulgaria Grade 1 stands as teaching cm, kg, euro/eurocent and the hour, not the litre.** Book errors proper (wrong mathematics) were found nowhere in the pages read; contents-page slips (e.g. Grade 4 Part 1 listing its angle lessons out of order in the contents) are apparatus slips, not teaching errors, and are counted nowhere. The placements' one NEW LESSON NEEDED topic — calculator use (Grade 4 Part 2, lessons 118–119, item 121) — is stocked in the existing `money-and-time/calculator-use` lesson built by the Sri Lanka expansion merge; no second calculator lesson is built. Into this lesson the placements stock 9 items (items 2, 20, 25, 26, 40, 41, 74, 75, 93).

**Book 1:** *Математика за 1 клас* (pupil textbook, Grade 1) — Bulgaria, Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika), 2nd edition 2026, ISBN 978-619-252-076-2; seven authors headed by Таня Иванова Вълкова; 50,686,679 bytes, 174 PDF pp.; printed folio = PDF page − 1 throughout the body. FULL BOOK (128 lessons, contents to printed p. 158 + 12 appendices). Pupil textbook.

## Djibouti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Djibouti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 document, the CRIPEN *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher's guide, series « À MOI LES MATHS », édition 2023; 129 pages); 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 68 teaching items. The document is a teacher's guide: for every lesson of the matching pupil manuel it states the headline objective, the pedagogical intention, the pupil objectives, the lesson conduct, the activities and the worked answers. Every Djibouti item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — the guide teaches teachers how to teach the topic; it is never a pupil textbook — cited as the evidence kind it is; Djibouti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Djibouti pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: the guide states (printed pp. 9–10) that since the 2019 school year teachers and pupils have a matching pupil manuel and livret of the same series (« À MOI LES MATHS », 5ème année), organised in the same four eight-week modules — those pupil books exist but are not in hand (placements record item R1), the guide's references to the manuel's pages are apparatus, not pupil content read here, and the student Maths files the library records locate are link-only partial extracts, never downloaded; none is part of this read and none is cited. Printed page = PDF page throughout the guide. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught content stated in a lesson sheet's headline objective and pupil objectives, splitting a sheet where it teaches two contents that belong to different course lessons; the pedagogical apparatus around each sheet (situation conduct, activity-by-activity commentary, exercise commentaries and synthesis questions) is not re-counted per activity. The placements' named exclusions — the avant-propos, préambule and présentation (printed pp. 3, 8–12: the competency approach, the four “moments of study”, the teacher's role, evaluation and remediation apparatus), the sommaire itself (printed pp. 4–7; its faults are placements record items R2 and R4), the mental-calculation routines (PLM) opening most sheets, per-sheet conduct and commentary counted once per content, and the guide's cross-references to the pupil manuel and livret pages — are stocked nowhere; the one content-bearing statement in the front matter, the end-of-primary objective's domain list (printed p. 9), is placed as a programme item in the ratio-and-proportion lesson. The Module 2 sheet at printed pp. 55–56 is a near-verbatim reprint of the Module 1 sheet at printed pp. 14–15 (placements record item R3); its content is placed once, as item 1. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide, Grade 5) — Djibouti, CRIPEN (Ministère de l'Éducation Nationale et de la Formation Professionnelle), édition 2023, series « À MOI LES MATHS »; 1,494,038 bytes, 129 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

## Finland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Finland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **24 files / 23 distinct works of government-hosted open material (OPH and the aoe.fi Library of Open Educational Resources); Finland has NO national textbook series** — and placed 173 items (51 pupil + 122 teacher evidence). The records' kind labels are wrong for most of the set: 13 of the 18 Mathematics-labelled “student” files are actually teacher-facing (in-service course books, theory texts, activity series and instructor guides), and only 6 files are pupil-facing (the Metsämatikkaa cards, the quadrilateral chart, the weather game, the Päivänselvää chapter, the programming workbook and its model solutions) — every book below is carried at its TRUE kind, never the record label. One byte-identical duplicate (aoe.2674-alku = aoe.2686-alku, the same file under two aoe records) is cited once; the Päivänselvää book is a Chapter-2-only excerpt, cited only for what it holds; the quadrilateral chart's filed copy is 1 content page + 175 blank padding pages. The placements' named exclusions — the weather game's non-temperature content, the computing-pedagogy matter in the two Turku course books, the motivation and mindset material in the JoMa books, the Pikkumatikoita parts 2, 8 and 11, and the programming books' pure-programming tasks — are stocked nowhere. Pupil items are COUNTED normally; teacher-evidence items are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count. The Grade 1 Näppituntuma volume's appendices (printed pp. 37–41: number cards 1–100 and a hundred chart, dot cards, and < = > sign cards) are apparatus for its placed items and are stocked nowhere as content. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 pupil items and 12 teacher-evidence items from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4, Book 5, Book 6 and Book 7.

**Book 1:** *Näppituntuma — Alkuopetuksen matematiikkaa toiminnallisesti* (Grade 1 teacher book) — Finland, Opetushallitus (OPH) / Oulun Matikkamaa, Junttila & Ristola, 2011; 1,009,501 bytes, 43 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an OPH supplementary teacher activity collection, expressly “not tied to any textbook”; Finland has no national textbook series.

**Book 2:** *Näppituntuma 2.lk* (Grade 2 teacher book) — Finland, Opetushallitus (OPH) / Oulun Matikkamaa, Junttila & Ristola, 2012; 1,980,041 bytes, 65 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an OPH supplementary teacher activity collection, expressly “not tied to any textbook”; Finland has no national textbook series.

**Book 3:** *Metsämatikkaa 1* (pupil outdoor task cards, aoe.1525) — Finland, Susanna Kaitera, 2021, CC BY-SA 4.0; 4,791,957 bytes, 14 PDF pp. (12 numbered task cards; cards 13–14 are a blank template and a completion record sheet). Printed page = PDF page (each card numbered). PUPIL material — counted.

**Book 4:** *Metsämatikkaa — ohje opettajalle* (teacher instruction for the Metsämatikkaa cards, aoe.1525) — Finland; 61,411 bytes, 1 p. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — apparatus instructions for the pupil cards; no new pupil content.

**Book 5:** *Mätning* (teacher theory text, in Swedish, aoe.2454) — Finland, Manninen & Filppa, University of Oulu; 105,511 bytes, 6 PDF pp.; cited by PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — a university text on teaching measurement in early-childhood and preschool education; the record labels it “student”.

**Book 6:** *Päivänselvää Matematiikka — Lisämateriaali, Luku 2* (pupil supplementary chapter, aoe.5681) — Finland, Nikkinen & Toivonen, Opike, 2025; 6,267,778 bytes, 44 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page + 8 (printed pp. 9–51). PUPIL material — counted. CHAPTER 2 ONLY (“Understanding mathematical relationships”) — a partial excerpt of the full supplementary book, complete as a chapter; cited only for what it holds.

**Book 7:** *Lumikuorma — Työpajan ohjaajan opas* (workshop instructor guide, aoe.4480) — Finland, LUMA Centre Lapland; 558,333 bytes, 3 PDF pp.; cited by PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — the record says “student”, grade-05; the document is an instructor's guide addressing grades 5–7. Book error carried corrected: PDF p. 3 gives a small car's average mass as “40 g” — impossible for a car; taught corrected (a small car is of the order of 1,000 kg). Its worked snow example (1.77 kg ÷ 0.0177 m² = 100 kg/m²) is correct.

Finland is added to the countries line and to the acceptance count above — 3 pupil items in this lesson, from Finland's pupil-facing open material. Its 12 teacher-evidence items in this lesson are named in the rows and stay outside the count. Finland's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** for the in-hand set — every in-hand Finland Maths file (24 files / 23 distinct works, including the 3 maths-content files filed under other subject labels) has been read in full and stocked — with the standing caveat: the set is OPH/aoe.fi supplementary open material, not a national series, and it is teacher-heavy (only 6 of the 24 files are pupil-facing).

## Dominica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Dominica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **4 student books, 0 teacher guides**, all by Raymond Toolsie (Caribbean Educational Publishers, CEP): *Mathematics Course for Secondary Schools, Book 3, New Edition* (Grade 9), *Mathematics: A Complete Course with CXC Questions, Volume One*, 2nd ed. (Grade 10) and *Volume 2* (Grade 11), and *Pure Mathematics: A Complete Course for CAPE, Unit 1* (Grade 12) — 2,335 PDF pages in all — and placed 460 teaching items. All four books are scanned-image PDFs with no text layer, read visually: the complete contents of every book read page by page, the opening pages of every chapter read in full, further section openings and worked-example pages read in order throughout each book, and the end matter (answers, indexes, back covers) checked for completeness; the placements are at the books' own section grain — one item per distinct taught topic — and every page cited is the book's printed page. Set facts carried exactly: the flipbook source labelled the Grade 9 book “Book 2” — the file itself is **Book 3, New Edition** (cover and copyright page, ISBN 978-976-648-123-4) and it is cited as Book 3 throughout; the 2026-10-22 “survival verification” copies recorded in ~/workspace/scribd_pull/ no longer exist (checked 2026-11-10) — the books themselves are safe: three sit in phase3-staging, and Volume One, whose staging copy is missing, was read from a byte-exact copy landed via the authenticated GitHub route and deleted after the read. One book-internal promise is NOT confirmed: Volume 2's Chapter 15 opener lists standard scores, but its contents names no such section (Standard Deviation, printed p. 892, is the last statistics section) — standard scores are stocked nowhere as a separate item. The placements' named exclusions — the chapter Revision exercises, the CXC/CSEC Past Paper Questions and Model Examinations, Volume 2's SBA guidelines section (printed pp. 1225–1238, coursework administration), the Three Figure Tables, the Answers, the Indexes and the Prefaces — are stocked nowhere. Book errors found in the read are carried corrected on the rows they touch. In the sections below the books are cited as Books 1–4 in the placements' order. Dominica COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of these student-book rows. Dominica's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (4/4) has been read and placed, on the in-hand definition.

Into this lesson the placements stock 2 items from Book 2.

**Book 2:** *Mathematics: A Complete Course with CXC Questions, Volume One*, 2nd edition (student book, Grade 10) — Dominica, Raymond Toolsie, Caribbean Educational Publishers (CEP), November 2004, ISBN 976-8014-16-43 (old-style ISBN as printed); 100,014,865 bytes, 723 PDF pp. Scanned-image PDF with no text layer, read visually; printed page = PDF page − 11 (±1 at divider leaves). The file is missing from phase3-staging; it was read from a byte-exact copy landed via the authenticated GitHub route (release mid-size-books-2; MD5 00925eeeeebbf1a4ef6997c9438fa271) and deleted after the read.

## Dominican Republic added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read the Dominican Republic's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 books, all of them student books (0 teacher guides exist in hand)**: the 11 dedicated *Matemática* books of MINERD's Proyecto Libro Abierto, Serie 1 (*Matemática* 2°–6° Primaria and *Matemática* 1°–6° Secundaria), plus the mathematics content of the 3 integrated *Primero* volumes (Grade 1 has no dedicated *Matemática* book; only the three volumes' Matemática blocks are placed) — 3,184 PDF pages in all — and placed 661 teaching items (614 from the dedicated books, 47 from the Primero volumes). Every book was grabbed fresh by the authenticated GitHub API route, verified byte-exact against its staging `books.json` byte count and cover/title-proved, and the grabbed copy deleted after reading; the recorded Google Drive fallback is login-walled from this host and delivered no book. The expansion map's “16 student” count is wrong as a count of *Matemática* books: the dedicated set is 11, and 16 is reachable only by adding integrated all-subject files that are not *Matemática* books. Printed page = PDF page in every book (verified on the unit dividers and page footers). The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught topic — a named section or a coherent teaching block inside a unit. The placements' named exclusions — the apparatus of all 14 books (unit-opening, group-activity, evaluation, self-assessment, bibliography and book-care pages, except the five group activities that teach a topic found nowhere else in their book, which are placed); the *Primero Volumen I* Matemática diagnostic evaluation; the Primero competency pages; the Primero volumes' non-maths sequences (with two maths-adjacent blocks inside them named in the placements and stocked nowhere); the 5 Nivel Inicial integrated books (a scope note, not part of the Maths set); and one record artefact that is not a book (a stray partial-upload `.part` file in the grade-04 push state) — are stocked nowhere. One book error is carried corrected on the row it touches: *Matemática 3° Primaria*'s weight table (evaluation, printed p. 152; the same table family at p. 147) pairs 8 lb with 48 oz — wrong: 1 lb = 16 oz, so 8 lb = 128 oz (48 oz is 3 lb). Minor slips, no action: the contents lists of *Matemática* 2° and 3° Secundaria run one page behind the printed folios — every citation uses the folios. Checked and NOT an error: *Matemática 4° Secundaria*, printed p. 84, prints Pythagoras as a² = b² + c² with a defined as the hypotenuse — correct as printed. In the sections below the books are cited as Books 1–14 in the placements' order. The Dominican Republic COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of these student-book rows. The Dominican Republic's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (the 11 dedicated *Matemática* books plus the Primero volumes carrying Grade 1's maths) has been read in full and placed.

Into this lesson the placements stock 33 items from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4, Book 5, Book 6, Book 9 and Book 14.

## Guyana added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Guyana's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 book, a student (pupil) textbook: *Let's Do Mathematics Book 5* (Ministry of Education, Georgetown, Guyana — Easy Path Series), the Grade 5 book; 0 teacher guides** — and placed 140 teaching items. The expansion map's count of 1 student + 0 teacher is CORRECT, proved from five records (textbooks.tsv, phase3-push-state.json, phase3-push-state-lane2.json, the staging books.json files and download-lists/guyana.md): Guyana has 6 books in hand in total and exactly one is Mathematics. The other five in-hand books (G1 *Science Around Us Book 1*, G1 *Social Studies For Our Children Book 1*, G2 *Fun With Language Book 2 Part 1*, G3 *Fun With Language Book 3*, G6 *Science Around Us Book 6*) were each byte-verified against their records and their covers/title pages read — each is the book its record names; **no maths book is hiding under another subject label**. All other Guyana Maths books in the records (Easy Path Books 1–4 and 6, the nursery numeracy workbooks and the “Mathematics Made Easy” series) are link-only — exact URLs recorded, no file in hand — and were not read. The one book was read from staging in place: 5,990,840 bytes (= the books.json record), 169 PDF pages, MD5 c6128b33f8842397e0351776a7f36929, and it is the COMPLETE book, not a partial excerpt — the contents page (PDF p. 7) lists all 30 units, every unit start was verified at its contents page, and the text runs printed pp. 1–158, ending with the final “Let Us Look Back” exercises (printed p. 158) and the back cover. Printed page = PDF page − 8 throughout (Unit 1 begins printed p. 1 = PDF p. 9). Book observation F1, carried exactly: the cover reads “Revised Edition 2004” (the staging books.json and the checklist follow the cover) while the imprint page (PDF p. 2) reads “© 2005 … Revised 2005” — one consistent scan of one book, so the edition is cited as “Revised Edition 2004 (cover); imprint © 2005”. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught skill or concept, at the point the book teaches it. The placements' named exclusions — front matter (acknowledgements, foreword, preface, contents; PDF pp. 1–8); every end-of-unit REVIEW section and every “LET US LOOK BACK” evaluative set (after Units 5, 10, 15, 20, 25 and at the end of Unit 30, printed pp. 156–158 — revision and assessment exercises that restate items already placed, no new skill taught); worked story problems and exercise sets inside the units (practice of the item taught on that page, not separate items); and the example exchange rates themselves (dated examples that differ between Units 20 and 21 by design; the taught skill stocked is the conversion method) — are stocked nowhere. Guyana COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of these pupil-book rows. Into this lesson the placements stock 10 items from Book 1 (items 35, 36, 37, 38, 98, 99, 102, 103, 116, 117).

## Gabon added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Gabon's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **17 documents: 6 pupil textbooks (the CIAM *Mathématiques* volumes for 6e, 5e, 4e, 2e Scientifique, 1re SM and 1re Littéraire, EDICEF), 6 teacher guides (the *Super Efficace* guides pédagogiques for 1re–5e année, EDIG/EDICEF, and the CIAM *Mathématiques 2e S* guide pédagogique) and 5 curriculum documents (the IPN *Curriculum Mathématiques* for 1re–5e année)** — every file grabbed fresh for the read, its byte size and page count matching the records exactly and its identity proved from cover/title/sommaire pages — and placed 522 items: 83 pupil-textbook teaching items, 389 teacher-evidence items and 50 curriculum-requirement items. The expansion map's total of 17 files is right but its split is wrong in kind (the read's flagged claim F1): the map lists “11 student + 6 teacher”, counting the 5 IPN curriculum PDFs among the student books; the true split is 6 pupil textbooks + 6 teacher guides + 5 curriculum documents. **No *Super Efficace* pupil book is in hand at any primary grade** — the primary pupil books are print-only commercial (EDIG) and were never secured, so Gabon's primary coverage is the IPN curriculum plus the teacher guide for each year; each guide teaches every lesson of the absent pupil book and cites its pages, and the guide rows below cite both the guide page and the pupil-book page the guide gives. There are no partial volumes and no duplicates in the set: every CIAM scan runs from its first chapter to its own index at the sommaire's stated page (the 6e scan opens at printed p. 3 — the cover and title leaves are not in the scan, but all teaching content is present, printed pp. 3–222 = the PDF's 220 pages exactly). The two misleading historic Drive filenames are settled by the books' own title pages: the file named “2nde SM” is the CIAM 2e Scientifique pupil book, and the file named “2nde C” is its 2e S Guide pédagogique. Link-only CIAM volumes (3e, Terminale SM/SE and their guides) are not in hand and are cited nowhere. Counting grain: one curriculum item per palier × compétence statement; one guide item per lesson the guide teaches; one pupil item per sommaire chapter. Book errors are carried corrected where they land: the Curriculum 2e année, Compétence 2, Palier 1 prints “le passionnément des objets l'espace”, a typo for “positionnement” (positioning of objects in space), and is stocked corrected; the Curriculum 3e année, Compétence 1, Palier 2 states “nombres de 1001 à 1999 (0 à 1999)” — the parenthetical contradicts the main range, and the statement is stocked by its main range; the CIAM 4e index spells PPMC as “PPCM” (trivial, noted for the record). The placements' named exclusions — the guides' remediation/application apparatus between paliers; the curricula's front matter and framework apparatus (rationale, timetables, competency-framework and assessment sections); the CIAM pupil books' end-of-chapter exercise sets, préfaces, authors' letters and closing indexes; the 2e S pupil book's Greek-alphabet page; the 2e S guide's synoptic programme tables — are stocked nowhere. Gabon COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its pupil-textbook rows; its teacher-evidence and curriculum-requirement rows are named in the table but stay outside the count. Into this lesson the placements stock 34 items (1 pupil-textbook, 32 teacher-evidence, 1 curriculum-requirement) from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4, Book 5, Book 6, Book 7.

Pages cited are the PDF pages; each requirement is cited by compétence and palier, as the placements give it.

## Ireland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Ireland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 pupil textbooks of the Text & Tests series (The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon): one English volume, Text & Tests 4 (New Edition), and eight Irish-language (Téacs & Trialacha) volumes** — and placed 570 items. Ireland has no government-published textbooks: every book in the set is a commercial in-use title held as a complete free copy. The English TT4 was read in phase3-staging (byte- and SHA-256-verified in place); the eight Irish volumes were re-grabbed from their recorded COGG (cogg.ie) source URLs, each landing byte-exact at its recorded size, every file's identity proved from its cover, copyright and contents pages. The expansion map's count is right: **9 student + 0 teacher** — the one further Ireland file in hand, *Dúchas 2 — Leabhar Scileanna*, is a Junior Cycle Irish-language (Gaeilge) skills book, not Maths, and is cited nowhere in this course. Extent, honestly: five whole-course books (TT1, TT2 GL, TT3, English TT4, TT6); three strand volumes complete as published but covering one strand only (TT4 Irish = Strand 2; TT5 Irish = Strand 1; TT7 Irish = Strand 5); and one supplement booklet (the TT5 Forlíonadh — deferred Strand 1 material and the Central Limit Theorem), complete as published, not a standalone textbook. Two record flags ride with the set (the read's flagged claims F1–F2, both about the library records, not the course): **F1** — 8 of the 9 PDFs are absent from their phase3-staging folders, which hold only books.json + CURRICULUM.md; all eight are secured in GitHub and were re-grabbed byte-exact from COGG for the read, and restoring them into staging is owed on the library side. **F2** — the held TT3 is the updated edition (2024) of the 2013 book, with an added Chapter 19, Inferential Statistics, that no record mentions; its three items are stocked from that chapter and TT3's book entry names the edition wherever it appears. The older strand books and the English TT4 New Edition teach much of the same Leaving Certificate Higher Level ground in two languages and editions (e.g. TT6 chs. 1–2 ≈ TT4 Eng chs. 1–2; TT7 ch. 2 ≈ TT4 Eng ch. 7): each book's row is stocked as its own citation — they are distinct held books, not duplicates — while Ireland itself is counted once in this lesson's taught-in figure. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of each book. The placements' named exclusions — every book's chapter tests (“Cuir triail ort féin”), tasks, learning-experience and review sections, chapter summaries and Answers sections, and every book's prefaces, keyword lists and contents pages — are stocked nowhere. Ireland COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Ireland book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 item, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (TT1).

**Book 1:** *Téacs & Trialacha 1 (Eagrán Nua)* — TT1 (pupil textbook) — The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon; Irish edition of *Text & Tests 1: First-Year Maths* by Paul Cooke, O.D. Morris and Deborah Crean (English ©2018), ISBN 978-1-9997795-3-5; 17,831,033 bytes, 423 PDF pp. Junior Cycle, 1st year. Printed page = PDF page − 7.

## Fiji added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Fiji's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 pupil textbooks of the Ministry of Education / Curriculum Development Unit series, one for each of Years 1–13** — and placed 1,012 items. The expansion map's count is exact: staging `books.json` holds exactly 13 Mathematics entries (one per year, all `kind: student`) and `phase3-push-state.json` lists all 13 as pushed; no Maths teacher guide is in hand (the AQEP Maths Guides for Lower Primary exist only as AnyFlip reader copies in the download list — not in hand, not counted). The staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, so each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) where the pusher had stored it as a blob, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (a Wayback Machine capture of the ministry file, Beacon Media, or Google Drive) — and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. The read's nine flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) `textbooks.tsv` carries only 9 of the 13 rows (Years 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13) — Years 5, 6, 8 and 9 are staged and pushed but missing from the TSV; (2) the Year 2 scan duplicates printed p. 57 and printed p. 58 is absent, so the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand to read and is cited nowhere; (3) the Year 5 capture has no cover/title leaves — the file opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, Strand 1 p. 1 to Strand 5 p. 98, its identity resting on the staged record and the body's own structure; (4) Year 4's folio sequence is irregular — folios 44–48 and 54 never appear and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice — but the leaf count closes exactly (85 printed numbers − 6 absent + 2 duplicated = 81 body leaves), every contents strand and unit is present and no activity breaks mid-stream at the gaps, so the evidence points to the printed book's own folio misprints rather than missing leaves, a judgement the file alone cannot prove; (5) Year 3's scan order is scrambled at the end (contents folio ii and printed pp. 46, 51, 52 sit at PDF pp. 76–80) and one activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51 — counted once), with folio tracking accounting for all of printed pp. 1–75; (6) Year 10 exists in two packagings — the staged Beacon Media file (9,099,595 bytes, 202 pp.) is the copy cited; an ECopies capture of the same book is 9,079,205 bytes / 200 pp.; (7) Year 12's first Wayback transfer arrived truncated (11,794,148 bytes, broken cross-reference table) and was discarded — a fresh download delivered the complete book (17,085,310 bytes, 284 pp.), which is what was read; (8) Year 1's contents lists Chance at p. 65 and Data at p. 66 where the folios are 64 and 65 — the body count closes exactly, so nothing is missing and the contents is one out; (9) Year 6's interior copyright and acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy-paste error in the book itself; its cover metadata, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout, and it is read and placed as Year 6. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach — among them Year 3's conversion-table lines “1,000 cm = 1 km” (correct: 100,000 cm) and “10,000 mm = 1,000 m” (correct: 10 m), Year 4's banner “30 minutes = 1 hour” against its own body's 60, Year 5's “1 000 millimetres = 1 litre”, Year 9's contents “Common Multitudes” (for Common Multiples) and its (a × b)ᵐ = aᵐ × bⁿ misprint, and Year 1's count-in-twos strip printing 9 where 19 belongs; Year 10 carries its own printed errata list (pp. 201–202), whose corrections are noted in the items. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — strand divider pages; contents and acknowledgement pages; pure exercise sets and worked-example repeats of an item already placed; Year 1's “Checking my number” ten-frame reference page (printed p. 32); Year 3's duplicated capacity page (counted once); Year 4's closing multiplication-table chart (printed p. 86); Year 10's printed errata list (pp. 201–202, apparatus — its corrections ride in the items); Year 12's closing motivational quotes and exam tips (printed pp. 273–275); Year 13's bibliography and back matter — are stocked nowhere. Fiji COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Fiji book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 58 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Mathematics Year 1*), Book 2 (*Mathematics Year 2*), Book 3 (*Mathematics Year 3*), Book 4 (*Mathematics Year 4*), Book 5 (*Year 5 Mathematics*), Book 6 (*Year 6 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 7 (*Year 7 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 8 (*Mathematics Year 8*), Book 9 (*Mathematics for Year 11*).

Printed page = PDF page − 2. Scan defect: printed p. 57 appears twice (PDF pp. 59–60 are the same leaf) and printed p. 58 is absent — the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand and is cited nowhere.

## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 18 items — 13 pupil-textbook rows and 5 teacher-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 3er grado*), Book 2 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 4to grado*), Book 7 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 1er grado*), Book 8 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 2do grado*), Book 9 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas and 4to grado*).

**Book 1:** *Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 3er grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación (the PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support); 16,407,713 bytes, 202 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 6. Pupil textbook.

**Book 2:** *Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 4to grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación (PROMETAM / UPNFM series, JICA support); 11,628,453 bytes, 172 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 6. Pupil textbook.

**Book 7:** *Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 1er grado* (mirror “web” edition) (teacher guide) — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; 23,910,414 bytes, 230 PDF pp. Teacher evidence only. A second scan of the same work — the official 2014 host copy (GD-MAT1-2014-WEB, 31,199,595 bytes, 230 pp.) — is also held: a duplicate-edition record (placements item 242), cited once with this book, not as a separate work; its text layer is materially poorer than this scan's.

**Book 8:** *Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 2do grado* (mirror “web” edition) (teacher guide) — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; 18,851,395 bytes, 222 PDF pp. Teacher evidence only. A second scan of the same work — the official 2014 host copy (GD-MAT2-2014-WEB, 26,308,871 bytes, 222 pp.) — is also held: a duplicate-edition record (placements item 241), cited once with this book, not as a separate work.

**Book 9:** *Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 4to grado* (teacher guide) — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; 19,799,179 bytes, 226 PDF pp. Teacher evidence only.

## Italy added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Italy's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 pupil textbooks, all adopted commercial titles (Italy has no government textbooks; schools adopt titles under the AIE/MIUR adozioni system): the Bergamini *Matematica.blu* volumes (Zanichelli) and the Sasso *Colori della Matematica* Edizione Verde and Edizione Blu volumes and triennio modules A–G (Petrini / De Agostini Scuola)** — and placed 781 items. The expansion map's “14 student + 0 teacher” count and kind split are exact against the staging `books.json` files, `textbooks.tsv` and the push state; every file was verified against its record (byte size, and MD5 where the record carries one) and read in full. Counting grain: one item per numbered theory section of the book's own Indice/Sommario. Two extent faults ride with the set, honestly: the files filed as *Matematica.blu* Vol. 1 (Book 3) and *Matematica.blu 2.0* Vol. 5 (Book 4) are each only the SECOND PART of the volume — Vol. 1 opens at Capitolo 13 (printed p. 670) and its Capitoli 1–12 and G1–G4 are not in the file; Vol. 5 opens at Capitolo 25 (printed p. 1560) and its Capitoli 18–24 are not in the file — so both are named as partial volumes wherever they are cited, only their held content is placed, and the missing first parts are not in hand anywhere and are cited nowhere (the ground the Vol. 1 first part would have covered is supplied inside this set by Books 5–6 at Sasso depth, and the Vol. 5 first part's limits and continuity ground by Book 12, Modulo G). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the eBook-only units the books' own Indici mark “(solo nell'eBook)”; and the apparatus around the numbered theory sections in every book — chapter openings, exercise blocks, laboratories, *Realtà e modelli*, exam-preparation sections (*Verso l'esame*, *Verso l'INVALSI*, *Verso l'università*), glossaries, answer keys, formula sheets, and *Approfondimento* boxes that are not numbered theory sections. Into this lesson the placements stock 7 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Matematica.blu — Vol. 2*), Book 3 (*Matematica.blu — Vol. 1*, second part only), Book 5 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Verde, Vol. 1*) and Book 6 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Vol. 1*).

**Book 1:** Bergamini/Trifone/Barozzi, *Matematica.blu — Vol. 2: Algebra, Geometria, Probabilità* (Zanichelli, 2010; filed at Grade 10) — Italy; 12,160,155 bytes, 907 PDF pp., MD5 identical to the source record's. Complete as published: the volume opens at Capitolo 9 (printed p. 633), its pagination continuous from Vol. 1. Pupil textbook (adopted commercial title).

**Book 3:** Bergamini/Barozzi/Trifone, *Matematica.blu — Vol. 1* (Zanichelli, 2016; filed at Grade 9) — Italy; 54,032,035 bytes, 909 PDF pp., MD5 identical to the source record's. **Partial volume as held — the file is the volume's SECOND PART only:** Algebra Capitoli 13–21, Capitolo β and Geometry Capitoli G5–G11, its theory opening at printed p. 670; Capitoli 1–12 and G1–G4 are not in the file and are cited nowhere. Pupil textbook (adopted commercial title).

**Book 5:** Sasso/Zoli, *Colori della Matematica — Edizione Verde, Vol. 1* (Petrini / De Agostini Scuola, 2017, ISBN 9788849421729; filed at Grade 9) — Italy; 76,934,490 bytes, 802 PDF pp., MD5 identical to the source record's. Complete as published. Pupil textbook (adopted commercial title).

**Book 6:** Sasso/Zanone, *Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Vol. 1* (Petrini / De Agostini Scuola, 2017, ISBN 9788849421668; filed at Grade 9) — Italy; 93,032,683 bytes, 962 PDF pp., MD5 identical to the source record's. Complete as published. Pupil textbook (adopted commercial title).

## Mauritius added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Mauritius's whole in-hand Maths-content set from the files — **2 documents, and no pupil textbook among them**: (A) the *Numeracy — Grade 7, Part 1, Pupil's Worksheets* of the Foundation Programme in Literacy, Numeracy and Skills (© Mauritius Institute of Education 2025) — pupil worksheets, but only a **partial excerpt**: the file holds printed pp. 1–5, the first topic of Part 1 (“Let's Measure in practical situations”, length only), ending inside that topic's exercise set; and (B) the *Extended Programme Progress Book, Grades 7–9+* (Ministry of Education, Tertiary Education, Science and Technology), a record-of-progress book filed in the library records under the subject “Assessment”, whose Mathematics sections (printed pp. 25–30 for Grades 7–8, printed pp. 105–124 for Grades 9–9+) state the competencies a pupil must reach — **curriculum evidence only**: the Progress Book teaches nothing itself, and every Progress Book row below is worded as the requirement it is and is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The expansion map is WRONG in substance for Mauritius (flag F2): it recorded “1 student / 0 teacher” in hand, but the counted file is a supplementary worksheets excerpt, not a pupil textbook, and the Progress Book was not counted at all; the true in-hand Maths-content set is 0 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 1 pupil worksheets excerpt and 1 progress-record book. Flag F1: the GitHub record marks the worksheets `quarters: "full"` and the file is named `pupil-full-year.pdf`, but the file itself is 7 PDF pages carrying printed pp. 1–5 only — whatever else Part 1 contains is not in hand, and the excerpt is cited only for the pages actually held (both flags are record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records). All other Mauritius Maths books — the MIE primary and secondary Mathematics textbooks (exact links only) and Prevocational Mathematics Year 2 (ministry link only, extra-grid) — are link-only, not in hand and not read; the other in-hand Mauritius files (Road Safety Education Grades 1–6, *A Pictorial Primer — Say No to Tobacco*, Kreol Morisien textbooks, the Pre-Primary curriculum documents) are other subjects, verified by a full census of the Mauritius folders. The placements' named exclusions — Book A's front matter (cover, Numeracy Panel page, copyright); the Grade 7 colour-naming competency (no mathematics content as stated; the pattern competency it prepares for is counted at Grade 8); the Grade 9 “perform arithmetic operations mentally” process statement; the incomplete printed fragment “including algebraic fractions ( )” at Grade 9+; the Progress Book's non-Mathematics subject sections and its blank record apparatus; and record items R1–R3 (a Physical Education cross-reference, the front-matter GPA worked example, and the worksheets cover's external game link) — are stocked nowhere. The Progress Book's book errors are carried corrected on the rows they touch: the Grade 7 fraction list prints ½ twice (item 17); the H.C.F./L.C.M. word-problem requirement is stated a second time, misplaced inside §1.6 Decimals (item 94); three statements are truncated in the book — “involving brackets of the form”, the form never given (item 145), “a^n for n = −1, −2, −3 and rational” (item 153), and “or in the form” (item 216); §5.2 is headed “Pictograms and bar charts” but states only frequency-table work (item 151); π is printed as the Cyrillic letter “л” (item 172); and the quadratic rows are printed under the §3.5 Matrices heading with the general form garbled as “( a 2+b +c)”, read as ax² + bx + c (items 199–202). Mauritius's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-heavy)**: only the 10 taught items rest on pupil material, and that from a 5-page excerpt, so Mauritius is named in the countries line and in the rows below but is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Bahamas/Canada evidence precedent. Into this lesson the placements stock 31 items from Book A and Book B (10 teaching items from the worksheets excerpt and 21 curriculum-evidence items from the Progress Book).

**Book A:** *Numeracy — Grade 7, Part 1, Pupil's Worksheets (Foundation Programme in Literacy, Numeracy and Skills)* (pupil's worksheets) — Mauritius, Mauritius Institute of Education, ©2025; 1,698,337 bytes, 7 PDF pages, MD5 49bd8cd1ced6870bb0a6af9f4ec7e200. PARTIAL EXCERPT: the file holds printed pp. 1–5 only — the first topic of Part 1, “Let's Measure in practical situations” (length) — ending inside that topic's exercise set; there is no contents page and no other topic, despite the GitHub record's `quarters: "full"`. Printed page = PDF page − 2. TEACHING — the worksheets themselves teach and practise the topic; cited only for the pages actually held.

**Book B:** *Extended Programme Progress Book, Grades 7–9+* (progress record book) — Mauritius, Ministry of Education, Tertiary Education, Science and Technology (Mauritius Institute of Education property per its copyright notice); 1,354,101 bytes, 218 PDF pages, MD5 aff08e158c2ad9ee08dd1815251052be. Filed in the records under subject “Assessment” (kind workbook). Complete as published; only its Mathematics sections are placed here (Grades 7–8: printed pp. 25–30; Grades 9–9+: printed pp. 105–124). Printed folio = PDF page. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the book states the competencies a pupil must reach and records progress against them; it teaches nothing itself, and its rows are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

Each guide carries the full lesson plans for its half of the Grade 6 year — learning outcomes, lesson objectives, the taught subject matter with worked examples and answers, practice and review; Mathematics-1 covers the number / algebra / statistics / social-mathematics half of the year (Chapters 1–9) and Mathematics-2 the geometry / mensuration half (Chapters 1–10); the two files are two halves of one complete Grade 6 teacher-guide slot, not duplicates of each other and not parts of one file. Every Myanmar item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — the guides teach teachers how to teach the topic; neither book is a pupil textbook — cited as the evidence kind it is; Myanmar is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Myanmar pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: both guides' lesson plans follow a matching Grade 6 pupil textbook (the guides refer throughout to “the textbook”, its figures, examples and exercises — e.g. Mathematics-1 exercises 1.1–9.8); that pupil textbook exists but is not in hand (placements record item R1) — the guides' references to it are apparatus, not pupil content read here — and every Myanmar student Maths book the library records locate is an exact-link record only, never downloaded; none is part of this read and none is cited. The expansion map's count and kind split are exact (0 student + 2 teacher): textbooks.tsv carries exactly 8 Myanmar rows, all Grade 6 teacher guides, exactly two of them Mathematics; neither push-state names Myanmar (the books were secured pre-phase-3 into the textbooks repo's main tree) and phase3-staging holds no Myanmar directory, so the read copies were grabbed by the authenticated GitHub API route at the exact recorded byte sizes (Mathematics-1: 1,117,915 bytes; Mathematics-2: 1,312,022 bytes) and deleted after the read. Printed page = PDF page throughout both guides (the folio printed at the head of every page equals its PDF page). The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught content stated in a lesson plan's objective and teaching section, splitting a lesson where it teaches two contents that belong to different course lessons; the guides' shared pedagogy apparatus (student-centred method, Bloom's taxonomy, assessment, the I-T-P-R lesson stages, per-lesson introduction/practice/review conduct) is not counted per lesson, and exercise sets drawn from the matching pupil textbook are apparatus serving the lesson's own item, not separate content. ## Mozambique added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Mozambique’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **15 files: 14 pupil Matemática modules of the ministry’s PESD distance-learning programme (PESD I Módulos 1–5, studied by the 1º ciclo = grades 7–9; PESD II Módulos 1–9, studied by the 2º ciclo = grades 10–12) and 1 teacher guide, the *Manual do Professor — 1.ª Classe*, whose Matemática part (printed pp. 146–183) is teacher evidence** — all from the Ministério da Educação e Desenvolvimento Humano (MINEDH) through its Instituto de Educação Aberta e à Distância (IEDA) — and placed 233 items (227 pupil-textbook items and 6 teacher-evidence items). Mozambique’s basis, by the user’s 2026-11-02 ruling, is this ministry distance-learning (PESD) set, not the commercial print set: the modules are studied by cycle, not per grade, and are cited as distance-learning books wherever they appear. The expansion map’s “14 student” count is exact, but its “0 teacher” is wrong — the teacher guide is filed under subject “general” at grade-01 and carries a full Matemática part; the true in-hand set is 14 pupil + 1 teacher guide. The three staging `books.json` files, `textbooks.tsv` and `phase3-push-state.json` agree on exactly these 15 files; no staging PDF remained on local disk (pusher-secured, local copies deleted), so every file was fetched fresh from its recorded official source, ieda.gov.mz, and matched its recorded byte size exactly; all 15 MD5s are distinct — no duplicates — and the generically named PESD II file `Modulo-9.pdf` is proved by its title page to be Matemática Módulo 9. Printed-page basis: PESD I modules — printed page = PDF page − 1 (footer-verified); PESD II modules — printed page = the contents-page foliation; the *Manual do Professor* — printed page = PDF page. Counting grain: one item per lesson of a module where the lesson teaches one course topic; a lesson is split into two items where it teaches two clearly distinct course topics. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected, as the placements record them: PESD II Módulo 3’s cover subtitle wrongly repeats Módulo 1’s (“Conjuntos Numéricos e Cálculo Algébrico”) — its interior is exponential and logarithmic functions throughout — and its answer section is headed “Soluções Modulo 1” (sic) although it is Módulo 3’s own key; PESD II Módulo 9’s answer section is headed “Soluções Módulo 8” (sic) although it is Módulo 9’s own key; PESD II Módulo 5’s contents numbers two different lessons “Lição 4” (the linear-function revision, printed p. 43, and the modulus function, printed p. 55) — both lessons are placed, as items 177 and 178 of this read; and PESD I Módulo 4’s contents page omits its Lição 13, which exists in the body at printed p. 64 and is placed (item 96), while its Lições 14 and 15 carry identical stated objectives and are placed as two items as printed (items 97 and 98). The placements’ record items are apparatus, stocked nowhere: R1 — every PESD module’s closing preparation test (teste de preparação), answer key (chave de correcção / soluções) and bibliography; R2 — the *Manual do Professor*’s answer key to the 1.ª Classe pupil book (printed pp. 174–183; the pupil book itself is not in hand); R3 — the DGD Módulo 1 spot-check copy, fetched only to verify its subject identity. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: DGD (Desenho e Geometria Descritiva, PESD II Módulos 1, 3–12 — a separate named subject of the Mozambican curriculum with its own module series, interior-checked on Módulo 1 and excluded as such; Módulo 2 is not in hand at all); all other PESD subjects in hand (Biologia, Química, Física, Geografia, História, Inglês, Português, Francês, TIC, Agro-pecuária, Empreendedorismo, Educação Visual, Filosofia) — separate curriculum subjects, none a Matemática book in disguise; the *Manual do Professor*’s Língua Portuguesa and Educação Física parts (printed pp. 4–145 and 184–215); and the primary print-programme Matemática books (1ª–6ª, Edição revista 2025), which are link-only, not in hand and not read. Pupil items are COUNTED normally; the teacher-evidence items (T1–T6, from the one teacher guide) are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count.
